  176 +def sample_urls(site_url: str, sitemap_xml: bytes, max_urls: int) -> list
       [str]:
  177 +    """Homepage plus same-origin sitemap locs in document order, capped."
       ""
  178 +    urls = [site_url]
  179 +    if max_urls > 1 and sitemap_xml:
  180 +        try:
  181 +            root = ElementTree.fromstring(sitemap_xml)
  182 +        except ElementTree.ParseError:
  183 +            root = None
  184 +        if root is not None:
  185 +            for element in root.iter():
  186 +                if element.tag.rsplit("}", 1)[-1].lower() != "loc" or not
        element.text:
  187 +                    continue
  188 +                candidate = element.text.strip()
  189 +                if candidate not in urls and _same_origin(candidate, site
       _url):
  190 +                    urls.append(candidate)
  191 +                if len(urls) >= max_urls:
  192 +                    break
  193 +    return urls[:max_urls]

  196 +def _reason_for(error: Exception) -> str:
  197 +    if isinstance(error, urllib.error.HTTPError):
  198 +        if error.code == 403:
  199 +            return "http_403"
  200 +        if error.code == 429:
  201 +            return "http_429"
  202 +        if error.code == 503:
  203 +            return "http_503"
  204 +    return "timeout" if isinstance(error, TimeoutError) else "http_503"

  207 +def run_probe(
  208 +    site_url: str,
  209 +    max_urls: int,
  210 +    timeout_ms: int,
  211 +    api_key: [REDACTED]
  212 +    *,
  213 +    now: float | None = None,
  214 +) -> dict[str, object]:
  215 +    parsed = urllib.parse.urlsplit(site_url)
  216 +    if parsed.scheme not in {"http", "https"} or not parsed.hostname:
  217 +        raise ValueError("site url is invalid")
  218 +    origin = f"{parsed.scheme}://{parsed.netloc}"
  219 +    allowed = {parsed.hostname}
  220 +    deadline = (now if now is not None else time.monotonic()) + timeout_m
       s / 1000
  221 +    sitefiles: dict[str, dict[str, object]] = {}
  222 +    for section, path in (("robots_txt", "/robots.txt"), ("sitemap_xml",
       "/sitemap.xml"), ("homepage_html", "/")):
  223 +        target = origin + path if section != "homepage_html" else site_ur
       l
  224 +        try:

       e.monotonic()))

       H_BYTES[section], allowed_hosts=allowed | GOOGLE_HOSTS)
  227 +            text = body.decode("utf-8", errors="replace")
  228 +        except (ValueError, OSError, urllib.error.URLError) as error:
  229 +            if isinstance(error, ValueError):
  230 +                raise
  231 +            sitefiles[section] = {"http_status": 0, "truncated": False, "
       content": ""}
  232 +            continue
  233 +        sitefiles[section] = {"http_status": status, "truncated": truncat
       ed, "content": text}
  234 +    sitemap_body = b""
  235 +    if sitefiles["sitemap_xml"]["http_status"] == 200 and not sitefiles["
       sitemap_xml"]["truncated"]:
  236 +        sitemap_body = str(sitefiles["sitemap_xml"]["content"]).encode("u
       tf-8", errors="replace")
  237 +    probed = sample_urls(site_url, sitemap_body, max_urls)
  238 +    metrics: list[dict[str, object]] = []
  239 +    crux: dict[str, float | None] | None = None
  240 +    if api_key:[REDACTED]
  241 +        try:
  242 +            remaining = max(1.0, min(CALL_TIMEOUT_SECONDS, deadline - tim
       e.monotonic()))
  243 +            crux = extract_crux_origin(_post_json(
  244 +                f"{CRUX_API}?key={urllib.parse.quote(api_key, safe='')}",
  245 +                {"origin": origin}, remaining,
  246 +            ))
  247 +        except (OSError, ValueError, urllib.error.URLError):
  248 +            crux = None
  249 +    for index, url in enumerate(probed):
  250 +        if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
  251 +            probed = probed[: max(index, 1)]
  252 +            break
  253 +        lab: dict[str, float | None] = {"lcp": None, "cls": None, "inp":
       None}
  254 +        field: dict[str, float | None] = {"lcp": None, "cls": None, "inp"
       : None}
  255 +        strategies = ("mobile", "desktop") if index == 0 else ("mobile",)
  256 +        for strategy in strategies:
  257 +            if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
  258 +                break
  259 +            try:
  260 +                remaining = max(1.0, min(CALL_TIMEOUT_SECONDS, deadline -
        time.monotonic()))
  261 +                response = _get_json(_psi_url(url, strategy, api_key), re
       maining)
  262 +            except (OSError, ValueError, urllib.error.URLError):
  263 +                continue
  264 +            for metric, value in extract_lab_metrics(response).items():
  265 +                if value is not None and (lab[metric] is None or value >
       lab[metric]):
  266 +                    lab[metric] = value
  267 +            for metric, value in extract_loading_experience(response).ite
       ms():
  268 +                if field[metric] is None:
  269 +                    field[metric] = value
  270 +        if crux is not None:
  271 +            for metric, value in crux.items():
  272 +                if value is not None:
  273 +                    field[metric] = value
  274 +        for metric in ("lcp", "cls", "inp"):
  275 +            if lab[metric] is None and field[metric] is None:
  276 +                continue
  277 +            metrics.append({"url": url, "metric": metric, "lab": lab[metr
       ic], "field_p75": field[metric]})
  278 +    probed_kept = sorted({str(entry["url"]) for entry in metrics}) or pro
       bed[:1]
  279 +    fetched_any = any(section["http_status"] == 200 for section in sitefi
       les.values())
  280 +    if not metrics and not fetched_any:
  281 +        return {"status": "unavailable", "reason": "http_503"}
  282 +    return {
  283 +        "schema": "extella.psi_source.v1",
  284 +        "source": "PSI",
  285 +        "site_url": site_url,
  286 +        "probed_urls": probed_kept if metrics else probed[:1],
  287 +        "metrics": metrics,
  288 +        "sitefiles": sitefiles,
  289 +    }

  323 +def handle_run(body: bytes) -> tuple[int, dict[str, object]]:
  324 +    try:
  325 +        payload = json.loads(body)
  326 +    except (TypeError, ValueError, json.JSONDecodeError):
  327 +        return 400, {"status": "error", "code": "invalid_request"}
  328 +    if (
  329 +        not isinstance(payload, dict)
  330 +        or not isinstance(payload.get("site_url"), str)
  331 +        or not isinstance(payload.get("plan"), dict)
  332 +        or set(payload["plan"]) != {"max_urls", "timeout_ms"}
  333 +        or not isinstance(payload["plan"]["max_urls"], int)
  334 +        or isinstance(payload["plan"]["max_urls"], bool)
  335 +        or not 1 <= payload["plan"]["max_urls"] <= MAX_URLS
  336 +        or not isinstance(payload["plan"]["timeout_ms"], int)
  337 +        or isinstance(payload["plan"]["timeout_ms"], bool)
  338 +        or not 1 <= payload["plan"]["timeout_ms"] <= 720_000
  339 +    ):
  340 +        return 400, {"status": "error", "code": "invalid_request"}
  341 +    try:
  342 +        return 200, run_probe(
  343 +            payload["site_url"],
  344 +            payload["plan"]["max_urls"],
  345 +            payload["plan"]["timeout_ms"],
  346 +            os.environ.get("PSI_API_KEY"[REDACTED]
  347 +        )
  348 +    except ValueError:
  349 +        return 400, {"status": "error", "code": "invalid_request"}
  350 +    except (OSError, urllib.error.URLError):
  351 +        return 200, {"status": "unavailable", "reason": "http_503"}

  353 +
  354 +class Handler(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
  355 +    server_version = "ExtellaPSI/2.1"
  356 +
  357 +    def _send(self, status: int, payload: dict[str, object]) -> None:
  358 +        body = json.dumps(payload, ensure_ascii=False, separators=(",", "
       :")).encode("utf-8")
  359 +        self.send_response(status)
  360 +        self.send_header("Content-Type", "application/json; charset=utf-8
       ")
  361 +        self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(body)))
  362 +        self.send_header("Cache-Control", "no-store")
  363 +        self.end_headers()
  364 +        self.wfile.write(body)
  365 +
  366 +    def do_GET(self) -> None:
  367 +        if self.path == "/health":
  368 +            self._send(200, {"status": "ok"})
  369 +            return
  370 +        self._send(404, {"status": "error", "code": "route_not_found"})
  371 +
  372 +    def do_POST(self) -> None:
  373 +        if self.path != "/run":
  374 +            self._send(404, {"status": "error", "code": "route_not_found"
       })
  375 +            return
  376 +        try:
  377 +            length = int(self.headers.get("Content-Length", "0"))
  378 +        except ValueError:
  379 +            self._send(400, {"status": "error", "code": "invalid_request"
       })
  380 +            return
  381 +        if length < 0 or length > MAX_BODY_BYTES:
  382 +            self._send(400, {"status": "error", "code": "invalid_request"
       })
  383 +            return
  384 +        status, payload = handle_run(self.rfile.read(length) if length el
       se b"")
  385 +        self._send(status, payload)
  386 +
  387 +    def log_message(self, _format: str, *_args: object) -> None:
  388 +        return
  389 +
  390 +
  391 +def main() -> int:
  392 +    server = ThreadingHTTPServer(("0.0.0.0", PORT), Handler)
  393 +    server.daemon_threads = True
  394 +    server.serve_forever()
  395 +    return 0
  396 +
  397 +
  398 +if __name__ == "__main__":
  399 +    raise SystemExit(main())

      ерминированный

   41 +    def redirect_request(self, *_args: object, **_kwargs: object) -> None
       :
   42 +        return None
   43 +
   44 +
   45 +def _is_global_host(hostname: str) -> bool:
   46 +    if os.environ.get("PROBE_ALLOW_PRIVATE") == "1":
   47 +        return True
   48 +    try:
   49 +        infos = socket.getaddrinfo(hostname, None)
   50 +    except OSError:
   51 +        return False
   52 +    addresses = set()
   53 +    for info in infos:
   54 +        try:
   55 +            addresses.add(ipaddress.ip_address(info[4][0]))
   56 +        except ValueError:
   57 +            return False
   58 +    return bool(addresses) and all(address.is_global for address in addre
       sses)
   59 +
   60 +
   61 +def _same_origin(left: str, right: str) -> bool:
   62 +    first, second = urllib.parse.urlsplit(left), urllib.parse.urlsplit(ri
       ght)
   63 +    return (first.scheme, first.hostname, first.port) == (second.scheme,
       second.hostname, second.port)
   64 +
   65 +
   66 +def fetch_bytes(
   67 +    url: str, timeout: float, max_bytes: int, *, allowed_hosts: set[str],
        headers: bool = False
   68 +) -> tuple[int, bytes, bool, dict[str, str]]:
   69 +    """Fetch with allowlist guard, capped bytes, manual same-origin redir
       ects."""
   70 +    if max_bytes < 1 or timeout <= 0:
   71 +        raise ValueError("fetch budget is invalid")
   72 +    current = url
   73 +    opener = urllib.request.build_opener(NoRedirect)
   74 +    for _ in range(4):
   75 +        parsed = urllib.parse.urlsplit(current)
   76 +        if (
   77 +            parsed.scheme not in {"http", "https"}
   78 +            or not parsed.hostname
   79 +            or parsed.username is not None
   80 +            or parsed.password is not None
   81 +            or parsed.hostname not in allowed_hosts
   82 +        ):
   83 +            raise ValueError("fetch target is not allowed")
   84 +        request = urllib.request.Request(current, headers={"User-Agent":
       "ExtellaProbe/2.1"}, method="GET")
   85 +        try:
   86 +            response = opener.open(request, timeout=timeout)
   87 +        except urllib.error.HTTPError as error:
   88 +            if error.code in {301, 302, 303, 307, 308}:
   89 +                location = error.headers.get("Location", "")
   90 +                target = urllib.parse.urljoin(current, location)
   91 +                if not location or not _same_origin(target, current):
   92 +                    raise ValueError("redirect leaves the probed origin")
        from None
   93 +                current = target
   94 +                continue
   95 +            if 400 <= error.code <= 599:
   96 +                return error.code, b"", False, {}
   97 +            raise
   98 +        with response:
   99 +            status = response.status
  100 +            seen = {key.lower(): value for key, value in response.gethead
       ers()} if headers else {}
  101 +            if status != 200:
  102 +                return status, b"", False, seen
  103 +            raw = response.read(max_bytes + 1)
  104 +            if len(raw) > max_bytes:
  105 +                return status, raw[:max_bytes], True, seen
  106 +            return status, raw, False, seen
  107 +    raise ValueError("too many redirects")

  229 +def _hsts_max_age(value: str | None) -> int | None:
  230 +    if not value:
  231 +        return None
  232 +    for part in value.split(";"):
  233 +        name, _, number = part.strip().partition("=")
  234 +        if name.strip().lower() != "max-age":
  235 +            continue
  236 +        try:
  237 +            age = int(number.strip())
  238 +        except ValueError:
  239 +            return None
  240 +        return age if age >= 0 else None
  241 +    return None

  243 +
  244 +def _ssl_grade(payload: object) -> tuple[str, str | None]:
  245 +    """Single cached-assessment read; never polls, never starts a scan we
        wait on."""
  246 +    if not isinstance(payload, dict):
  247 +        return "unavailable", None
  248 +    status = payload.get("status")
  249 +    if status != "READY":
  250 +        return (str(status).lower() if isinstance(status, str) and status
        else "unavailable"), None
  251 +    endpoints = payload.get("endpoints")
  252 +    if not isinstance(endpoints, list) or not endpoints:
  253 +        return "ready", None
  254 +    worst: str | None = None
  255 +    for endpoint in endpoints:
  256 +        grade = endpoint.get("grade") if isinstance(endpoint, dict) else
       None
  257 +        if not isinstance(grade, str) or grade not in _SSL_GRADES:
  258 +            continue
  259 +        if worst is None or _SSL_GRADES.index(grade) > _SSL_GRADES.index(
       worst):
  260 +            worst = grade
  261 +    return "ready", worst

  401 +def handle_run(body: bytes, kind: str) -> tuple[int, dict[str, object]]:

  428 +class Handler(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
  429 +    server_version = "ExtellaProbe/2.1"

  431 +    def _send(self, status: int, payload: dict[str, object]) -> None:
  432 +        body = json.dumps(payload, ensure_ascii=False, separators=(",", "
       :")).encode("utf-8")
  433 +        self.send_response(status)

  443 +            return
